# Graph Report - Reasoner  (2026-06-15)

## Corpus Check
- 2582 files · ~1,049,882 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 36286 nodes · 52139 edges · 2998 communities (2519 shown, 479 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 78% EXTRACTED · 22% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 11437 edges (avg confidence: 0.56)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `7c1df603`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
